# Review checklist: Madeleine Albright (albright)

Generated 2026-11-02 by tools/export.py (preview export).

This is a preview. Every block of wordpress.html is stamped "DRAFT — NOT REVIEWED"; do not publish it.

Facts: disputed 25, rejected 4, single-source 97, verified 78. Media: disputed 22, rejected 1, verified 73.

How to use this checklist: for each item, open the source link and find the quote, then tick one box (for an edit, write the new wording). Return the file to the maintainer, who records the decisions in review/decisions.jsonl and runs tools/apply_review.py. Approval does not override the checks: only verified items can be approved and published. Disputed, conflicting and single-source items can be edited (then checked again), rejected, or given a note.

## 11. Gaps (23)

Parts of the life story with no verified source yet. These are not errors.

- Family: no verified fact yet. Parents, marriage, children and the family's Jewish ancestry exist only as deferred candidates (staging/deferred-albright-2026-10-09-2: domain F-111, F-112; institutional F-122, F-130, F-149, F-150, F-152; nara F-901, F-902, F-107; media F-4).
- Education: no verified fact yet. Secondary school, undergraduate study, graduate degrees, thesis and dissertation exist only as deferred candidates (institutional F-101 to F-110, domain F-113, nara F-104); none is staged in facts.jsonl.
- Legacy: no verified fact yet. Papers at the Library of Congress and Wellesley, the Smithsonian donation and exhibition, the half-staff proclamation and the Senate tribute exist only as deferred candidates (institutional F-111 to F-113; smithsonian F-15, F-32 to F-34; nara F-106; domain F-124).
- Birth date: no verified source yet (ALB-F-0003 is disputed: its browser-fetched snapshot could not be confirmed live). Birth place is verified (ALB-F-0041, ALB-F-0080).
- Birth name variant 'Madlenka' (ALB-F-0025) is single-source.
- Death date: no verified source yet (ALB-F-0004 disputed). Age at death (ALB-F-0048) and cause of death (ALB-F-0049) are single-source. Only the place of death is verified (ALB-F-0005), and the profile carries it inside the honors section rather than a death or legacy section.
- Arrival in the United States: exact date and port of entry have no verified source yet (ALB-F-0064 and ALB-F-0082 disputed; ALB-F-0065 single-source; ALB-F-0063 on age at arrival disputed). Only the year is verified (ALB-F-0072).
- Earlier flight to Britain and the 1948 departure from Prague: no verified source yet (ALB-F-0085, ALB-F-0157, ALB-F-0042 disputed; ALB-F-0086 single-source); the family's return after the war (ALB-F-0114) is disputed.
- Asylum and settlement: political asylum (ALB-F-0012), teen years in Denver (ALB-F-0013) and the father's University of Denver post (ALB-F-0018) are single-source or disputed; ALB-F-0083 (father's United Nations job) is verified but no fact yet explains how the family came to settle in Denver.
- Citizenship: the year is verified (ALB-F-0026); 'during college' (ALB-F-0073) is single-source and the 50th-anniversary oath renewal (ALB-F-0084) is disputed. No naturalization record found (NARA and LoC newspaper searches for 1955-1963 returned nothing).
- Career, Secretary of State firsts: 'first female Secretary of State' (ALB-F-0007), '64th Secretary of State' (ALB-F-0024), the nomination date (ALB-F-0022), the swearing-in date (ALB-F-0068) and 'highest ranking woman' (ALB-F-0151) are all disputed; the profile cannot yet say she was the first woman in the post.
- Career, United Nations dates: appointment and credentials dates are unresolved (ALB-F-0101 disputed; ALB-F-0102, ALB-F-0103, ALB-F-0104 single-source) and do not agree with each other; resolve before any date is shown.
- Career, NATO enlargement detail: ALB-F-0089 to ALB-F-0092 are disputed; only the general claim ALB-F-0009 is verified.
- Career, other single-source or disputed roles: Defense Policy Board (ALB-F-0036, ALB-F-0139), Community of Democracies (ALB-F-0040), Wilson Center fellowship (ALB-F-0039), Georgetown Women in Foreign Service program (ALB-F-0098), CSIS fellowship (ALB-F-0100), Mondale-Ferraro campaign (ALB-F-0096), NSC legislative affairs (ALB-F-0097), World Refugee Council (ALB-F-0113).
- Awards and honors: National Women's Hall of Fame (ALB-F-0087, ALB-F-0088), Great Americans medal (ALB-F-0078) and Portrait of a Nation Prize (ALB-F-0079) are disputed; only the Order of the White Lion, Presidential Medal of Freedom (both in the profile) and the Transatlantic Leadership Award (ALB-F-0070, not in the profile) are verified.
- Books: Prague Winter (ALB-F-0115) and the 1983 Poland book (ALB-F-0045) are disputed; Read My Pins (ALB-F-0075) is single-source. Bestseller count: ALB-F-0037 (verified) and ALB-F-0154 (single-source) give different numbers; do not state a count beyond ALB-F-0037 until reconciled.
- Pins section rests on two facts (ALB-F-0136, ALB-F-0153); ALB-F-0152 on pins as a diplomatic signature is single-source, and the pin-diplomacy candidates (smithsonian F-14, F-37, F-38, F-47, F-52; media F-7) are deferred.
- IHH page claims with no independent source yet: occupation 'Politician', post-government roles as professor, author and advisor, 'seven books', 'public policy and memoir', Hanno R. Ellenbogen Citizenship Award (2000), Fulbright Prize for International Understanding (2001), the brooch collection, 'trailblazer in diplomacy' and 'lasting impact' (deferred baseline F-14, F-29 to F-33, F-36, F-37, F-39 to F-42). The fulbright.org prize page did not mention her.
- Name aliases: after the critic withdrew ALB-F-0019 to ALB-F-0021 (catalogue headings), the aliases 'Albright, Madeleine Korbel', 'Korbel, Madeleine', 'Korbel, Marie Jana' and 'Madeleine Korbel Albright' have no identity-fact evidence and are not used for name matching.
- Media with no usable licence: ALB-M-0019, ALB-M-0020, ALB-M-0024, ALB-M-0046 and ALB-M-0047 have no rights statement (G1 errors). ALB-M-0025 to ALB-M-0045 carry only a page copyright line for 2026, not a reuse licence. ALB-M-0009 (Herblock), ALB-M-0021 to ALB-M-0023 (Smithsonian terms) and ALB-M-0011 to ALB-M-0013 (rights assessment left to the user) need clearance before display. The profile has no artifacts.
- Single-source facts: 51 facts are single-source (G5), mostly NARA and State Department photo and travel records (ALB-F-0102 to ALB-F-0160); 35 are disputed, most because a browser-fetched snapshot could not be confirmed live (G10).
- Search families that found nothing: LoC Albright Papers finding aid (Cloudflare challenge, ALB-Q-0048, ALB-Q-0186; PDF ALB-Q-0185), LoC 2015 press release on the papers (403, ALB-Q-0049), state.gov biography and Diplomatic Reception Rooms (403, ALB-Q-0050, ALB-Q-0051), State Magazine 2022 (JavaScript-only, ALB-Q-0047), loc.gov catalog and contributor searches (ALB-Q-0055 to ALB-Q-0062, ALB-Q-0194), LoC newspapers 1948-1963 (ALB-Q-0202 to ALB-Q-0208), NARA name-variant and education searches (ALB-Q-0092, ALB-Q-0114 to ALB-Q-0119, ALB-Q-0125, ALB-Q-0132 to ALB-Q-0134), Archives of American Art and Smithsonian Institution Archives (ALB-Q-0149, ALB-Q-0151).
